- **data-isolation-reviewer**: Reviews server code for data-isolation mistakes — one user reaching another user's records by changing an id, and, in multi-tenant apps, one customer seeing another customer's data (queries that skip the tenant scope, a tenant id taken from request input, endpoints without a cross-tenant test). Learns how the project scopes data first. Use after any change to an API handler, service or database query, before the work is marked done. Read-only; reports findings, never edits. ([dsngeu/ThinkMap](https://github.com/dsngeu/ThinkMap/blob/HEAD/catalogue/agents/data-isolation-reviewer.md))
